Model before rewriting:

find x: int(2..4)
find y: int(2..4)
find z: int(2..4)

such that

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#direct_1: bool
  find z#direct_2: bool
  find z#direct_3: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3;int(1..)])

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#direct_1: bool
  find x#direct_2: bool
  find x#direct_3: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(x#direct_1),toInt(x#direct_2),toInt(x#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)])

   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])

  find y#direct_2: bool
  find y#direct_3: bool

  or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3;int(1..)])

--

product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15,
sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(x#direct_1),toInt(x#direct_2),toInt(x#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1,

or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3;int(1..)])
   ~~> select_cardinality ([("SAT", 20000)])
product([product([x,y;int(1..)]),z;int(1..)]) < 15,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(x#direct_1),toInt(x#direct_2),toInt(x#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1,
